### [1] [Geometry-Aware Representation Denoising for Robust Multi-view 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26230)
*Jin Hyeon Kim,Jaeeun Lee,Claire Kim,Kyoungjin Oh,Paul Hyunbin Cho,Jaewon Min,Yeji Choi,Jihye Park,Hyunhee Park,Minkyu Park,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GARD是一个在3D重建模型特征空间中直接进行扩散去噪的框架，能同时恢复3D几何和高质量RGB图像，在DA3基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建模型在理想无退化条件下表现优异，但真实场景中常存在各种图像退化（如噪声、模糊等），导致模型鲁棒性不足。本文旨在解决多视角3D重建在退化条件下的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 提出GARD框架，在3D重建模型的几何感知特征空间中执行基于扩散的多视角恢复，利用重建器自身的几何感知特征来恢复准确场景几何；同时通过额外的RGB图像解码器，从精化后的特征中恢复高质量RGB图像，实现几何与图像的联合恢复。

Result: 在Depth Anything 3 (DA3)基准上进行了全面实验，验证了GARD框架的有效性，能够同时恢复3D场景几何和高质量图像。

Conclusion: GARD通过在3D重建模型的特征空间中引入扩散去噪，有效提升了多视角3D重建在退化条件下的鲁棒性，并实现了几何与图像质量的同步恢复。

Abstract: Multi-view 3D reconstruction has achieved remarkable progress with the advent of feed-forward 3D reconstruction models. However, these models are typically trained and evaluated under ideal, degradation-free imaging conditions, whereas real-world observations often contain degradations that differ significantly from such settings. Improving robustness for multi-view 3D reconstruction under degraded conditions therefore remains an important challenge. We present Geometry-Aware Representation Denoising (GARD), a novel framework that performs diffusion-based multi-view restoration directly in the feature space of a feed-forward 3D reconstruction model. This design exploits the geometry-aware feature representations of the 3D reconstructor to effectively recover accurate scene geometry. Furthermore, by employing an additional RGB image decoder, the refined representations can also be used to restore high-quality RGB images, thereby enabling the simultaneous recovery of 3D scene geometry and high-quality imagery. Comprehensive experiments on the Depth Anything 3 (DA3) benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed GARD framework.

</details>


### [2] [Not All Modalities Are Equal: Instruction-Aware Gating for Multimodal Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26232)
*Bonan Ding,Umair Nawaz,Ufaq Khan,Abdelrahman M. Shaker,Muhammad Haris Khan,Jiale Cao,Jin Xie,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniMVU框架，通过指令感知的动态门控机制（内模态门控+模态级门控）实现多模态视频理解中的自适应融合，在六个基准上超越静态融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型在处理多模态输入（如音频、深度图等）时存在模态干扰问题，统一融合会导致无关通道影响模型性能。

Method: 提出UniMVU框架，包含：(1) 指令驱动的内模态门控模块，突出各模态内的显著区域；(2) 模态级门控模块，通过控制标记重新加权整个模态流；(3) 对于时间对齐流，采用快慢融合策略减少冗余。

Result: 在AVQA、AVSD、Music-AVQA、ScanQA、SQA3D和MVBench六个基准上取得一致提升，CIDEr指标最高提升13.5。

Conclusion: UniMVU提供了一种简单、统一的多模态视频理解方案，无需手工设计融合规则，门控机制与人类可解释的模态相关性一致。

Abstract: Pre-trained video large language models excel at visual reasoning. However, they struggle when videos arrive with auxiliary streams, such as audio, depth map, or dense temporal evidence. In such a scenario, uniform fusion induces modality interference, allowing irrelevant channels to distract the model. To address this issue, we present a unified multimodal video understanding framework, named UniMVU, that performs instruction-aware fusion across video, audio, depth map, or any other modality inputs via two levels of dynamic gating: inner-modality gates emphasize salient regions within each modality, whereas modality-level gates re-weight whole streams; both are conditioned on the text instruction to adaptively balance modality importance. Our UniMVU combines cross-modal self-attention with instruction-driven inner-modality gating module and a modality-level gating module with control token; for time-aligned streams we further adopt a fast-to-slow fusion scheme that reduces redundancy. Across six benchmarks (AVQA, AVSD, Music-AVQA, ScanQA, SQA3D and MVBench), our UniMVU achieves consistent gains over static-fusion baselines achieving gains as high as 13.5 in terms of CIDEr metric. Further, our analysis shows that the gating mechanism aligns with the human-interpretable modality relevance, and ablations show the contributions of inner-modality and modality-level gating. Our UniMVU provides a simple, unified recipe for instruction-aware multimodal video understanding that scales to diverse modalities without hand-crafted fusion rules.

</details>


### [3] [DuoGesture: Neuro-Inspired and Biomechanically Informed Dual-Stream Co-Speech Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26236)
*Ferdinand Paar,Lanmiao Liu,Aslı Özyürek,Serge Thill,Esam Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DuoGesture，一种双流神经模型，将共语手势生成分解为语义流和节拍流，通过语义变分信息瓶颈、运动-语义条件化和惯性节拍先验实现更优的语义表达和节奏一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有整体手势模型将词汇语义手势与韵律节拍手势混合处理，导致语义基础不足、语音-动作对齐不佳及运动平滑性受限。

Method: 双流架构：语义变分信息瓶颈（随机帧级门控控制语义覆盖节拍时机）、运动-语义条件化（用运动-语言表征替代纯词嵌入）、惯性节拍先验（人体测量加权臂链减少抖动）。

Result: 客观评估与主观实验均优于强整体基线，消融实验证实了语义基础、随机流选择和生物力学正则化的互补作用。

Conclusion: 受神经启发的双流分解方法能有效提升共语手势生成的语义表达与节拍一致性，且各组件贡献互补。

Abstract: Co-speech gesture generation requires both semantic expressivity and biomechanically plausible rhythmic motion. Existing holistic gesture models mix lexically grounded semantic gestures with frequent prosody-aligned beat gestures. This limits semantic grounding, speech-motion alignment, and kinematic smoothness. We propose \emph{DuoGesture}, a neuro-inspired and biomechanically informed dual-stream approach that decomposes co-speech gesture synthesis into coupled semantic and beat streams. The two streams are coordinated by a \emph{Semantic Variational Information Bottleneck}, a stochastic frame-level gate that learns when semantic gestures should override rhythmic beat motion. The semantic stream is controlled by \emph{Motion-Grounded Semantic Conditioning}, which replaces purely linguistic word embeddings with motion-language representations to provide motion-aligned semantic priors for long-tailed lexical triggers of gestures. The beat stream is further regularised by an \emph{Inertial Beat Prior}, an anthropometry-weighted arm-chain module that reduces jitter and improves rhythmic consistency without constraining semantic frames. Objective evaluations and subjective experiments show that DuoGesture outperforms strong holistic baselines, while component ablations confirm the complementary roles of semantic grounding, stochastic stream selection, and biomechanical regularisation.

</details>


### [4] [Sentinel: Embodied Cooperative Spatial Reasoning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26239)
*Xiangye Lin,Hongxin Zhang,Ruxi Deng,Qinhong Zhou,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了哨兵挑战（Sentinel Challenge）基准和CoSaR框架，用于研究城市规模户外环境中多智能体在动态约束下的协作空间智能。CoSaR结合大模型的通信规划能力与经典导航算法，使智能体通过自然语言协商安全集合点并避开动态哨兵，实验表明该方法在14个城市场景中显著提升了聚集速度、路径长度和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对城市规模户外环境中分散式具身智能体在动态约束下进行协作空间推理与规划的标准化评估基准，该问题对于现实多智能体协作系统至关重要。

Method: 提出CoSaR框架，将基础模型的高层通信与规划能力与经典空间导航算法的精确性相结合，使智能体能够交换情境更新、推理动态空间约束并协作重新规划轨迹。

Result: 在14个城市级场景中（3-5个智能体），CoSaR在聚集速度、路径长度和安全性方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 将动态通信与空间推理相结合是实现鲁棒多智能体协作的关键；通过形式化该新任务并提供可扩展基准，为推进具身多智能体系统中的协作空间智能奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this work, we study Cooperative Spatial Intelligence, the ability of decentralized embodied agents to coordinate effectively under dynamic environmental constraints across city-scale outdoor domains. We introduce Sentinel Challenge, a benchmark where multiple decentralized embodied agents must communicate in natural language to agree on a mutually safe and convenient meeting point within large, city-scale outdoor environments. Each agent must then navigate safely while avoiding dynamic sentinels patrolling the area, using a tool that provides coarse spatial information. To address this, we propose CoSaR (Cooperative Spatial Reasoning and Planning), a framework that bridges the high-level communication and planning abilities of foundation models with the precision of classical spatial navigation algorithms. CoSaR enables agents to exchange situational updates, reason over evolving spatial constraints, and collaboratively replan trajectories. Evaluated across 14 city-level scenes with 3-5 agents, CoSaR consistently leads to faster gathering, shorter path lengths, and improved safety. Our results demonstrate that integrating dynamic communication with spatial reasoning is essential for robust multi-agent cooperation. By formalizing this new setting and providing a scalable benchmark, we aim to build a foundation for advancing cooperative spatial intelligence in embodied multi-agent systems. Code and challenge are available at https://github.com/UMass-Embodied-AGI/Sentinel.

</details>


### [5] [RoMo: A Large-Scale, Richly Organized Dataset and Semantic Taxonomy for Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26241)
*Jiahao Zhang,Joseph Liu,Young-Yoon Lee,Seonghyeon Moon,Victor Zordan,Guy Tevet,Karen Liu,Stephen Gould,Oren Jacob,Haomiao Jiang,Mubbasir Kapadia,Yizhak Ben-Shabat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoMo是一个大规模、高质量、经过精心筛选的野外人体运动数据集，通过分层语义分类和过滤管道解决了现有数据集规模与质量不可兼得的问题，训练出的模型在保真度和多样性上达到SOTA，并配套发布了Motion Toolbox以促进可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体运动数据集存在两难选择：小规模高保真的动作捕捉数据 vs 大规模但充斥静态/低质量序列的野外数据，缺乏兼具规模与质量的数据集来驱动生成模型的发展。

Method: 提出RoMo数据集，采用基于分类感知的过滤管道（taxonomy-aware filtering pipeline）激进地去除静态和伪影序列；为每条序列标注详细描述，并按三级语义分类法组织数据，支持细粒度的逐类别评估。

Result: 基于RoMo训练的模型在保真度和多样性上达到当前最优，对复杂细微文本提示的理解能力显著提升；分层分类揭示了全局指标无法体现的模型优缺点。

Conclusion: RoMo解决了运动数据集规模与质量的矛盾，通过精细分类和标准化工具箱为可解释、可复现的运动生成研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Success in generative modeling across language, image, and video demonstrates that large, well-curated datasets are the key driver for building capable models. 3D Human motion, however, has lagged behind, constrained by an unsatisfying choice between small, high-fidelity motion capture datasets and large-scale in-the-wild collections dominated by static or low-quality sequences. We introduce RoMo, a rich, large-scale, carefully curated dataset of in-the-wild human motions that resolves these tradeoffs. To ensure quality, we introduce a taxonomy-aware filtering pipeline that aggressively removes static and artifact-prone sequences. Every sequence is annotated with detailed captions and organized by a novel three-level semantic taxonomy. This hierarchical structure enables fine-grained, per-category evaluation, that reveals model strengths and weaknesses obscured by global metrics. We demonstrate that models trained on RoMo achieve state-of-the-art fidelity and diversity while gaining a superior understanding of complex, subtle text prompts. Finally, we release the Motion Toolbox to standardize metrics, data conversion, and visualization, establishing a foundation for reproducible and interpretable motion generation research.

</details>


### [6] [LongAV-Compass: Towards Unified Evaluation of Minute-Scale Audio-Visual Generation Across T2AV, I2AV, and V2AV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26244)
*Tengfei Liu,Yang Shi,Xuanyu Zhu,Jiafu Tang,Liu Yang,Qixun Wang,Zhuoran Zhang,Yuqi Tang,Fengxiang Wang,Yuhao Dong,Xinlong Chen,Bozhou Li,Bohan Zeng,Yue Ding,Xiaohan Zhang,Jialu Chen,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Leye Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了LongAV-Compass，首个针对分钟级音视频生成的系统评测基准，涵盖多种输入模态和20+细粒度维度，通过11个模型实验验证了其诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频生成评测主要局限于5-10秒的短片段，缺乏对分钟级长内容的统一评测，且无法评估身份一致性、叙事连贯性和音视频对齐在长时间跨度下的退化问题。

Method: 构建了284个测试用例，覆盖T2AV、I2AV、V2AV三种模态，结合分类学引导的基准构建与统一评测框架，集成MLLM辅助评估及DINO-v2、ArcFace、CLIP、ImageBind等多种感知与多模态指标，评测20+细粒度维度。

Result: 在11个代表性模型上进行了实验，并结合人类对齐验证，证明该基准能够有效诊断当前系统在分钟级音视频生成中的局限性。

Conclusion: LongAV-Compass为分析当前系统在长时音视频生成中的连贯性、语义对齐和时间一致性提供了有效的诊断测试平台。

Abstract: Audio-visual generation is rapidly advancing from short clips to minute-long content, while existing evaluation protocols remain largely confined to short-form settings. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on 5--10 second text-conditioned generation and rarely support unified evaluation across text, image, and video conditioning modalities. Moreover, they provide limited insight into how identity consistency, narrative coherence, and audio-visual alignment degrade over extended temporal horizons. To bridge this gap, we introduce LongAV-Compass, a systematic benchmark for minute-long audio-visual generation. LongAV-Compass contains 284 curated test cases spanning text-to-audio-video (T2AV), image-to-audio-video (I2AV), and video-to-audio-video (V2AV), organized by application scenario and generation complexity. The benchmark combines taxonomy-guided benchmark construction with a unified evaluation framework that integrates MLLM-assisted assessment with complementary perceptual and multimodal metrics, including DINO-v2, ArcFace, CLIP, and ImageBind. The framework evaluates more than 20 fine-grained dimensions covering within-segment quality, cross-segment consistency, global narrative coherence, semantic alignment, and audio-visual synchronization. Through experiments on 11 representative models together with human-alignment validation, LongAV-Compass provides a diagnostic testbed for analyzing the limitations of current systems in sustaining coherent, semantically aligned, and temporally consistent minute-scale audio-visual generation across diverse input modalities.

</details>


### [7] [Dimensional Distribution Emotion State: Leveraging Valence and Arousal as a Common Embedding Space for Visual Emotion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26262)
*Émile Bergeron,Tadagbé Dhossou,Sébastien Tremblay,Jean-François Lalonde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的情感表征方法DDES（维度分布情感状态），用于预测艺术作品引发的情感反应，以辅助博物馆策展人设计基于情感的展览。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博物馆正在探索基于情感的展览方式以提升观众参与度和艺术民主化，但人工标注艺术品情感内容耗时费力且存在策展人个人偏见，因此需要自动化工具来预测艺术作品引发的情感反应。

Method: 利用连续二维情感空间来增强情感表征和深度学习模型训练过程；提出新的表征方法DDES（维度分布情感状态），并结合多数据集训练流程。

Result: DDES在保持与现有主流表征方法相当的基线性能的同时，提供了多项优势。

Conclusion: DDES作为一种新的情感表征方法，能够有效辅助博物馆策展人设计情感化展览，在性能不降低的前提下提供了更多灵活性。

Abstract: Museums are important sites for the dissemination of culture and art. They are institutions rooted in history and tradition; their exhibitions are often designed to highlight these aspects. Recently, a new approach is being explored in the field: emotion-based exhibitions. These exhibitions are designed specifically to elicit emotions in the visitors, in order to maximize engagement, and as a way to democratize access to art and attract a wider, more diverse audience. To do so, the emotional content of the artworks must first be extracted, however, manually annotating the artworks by experts is a prohibitively labor-intensive process, and risks introducing the personal bias of curators. To assist the museum curators in their design of these exhibitions, we wish to develop a tool that can predict the emotional response evoked by a work of art. In this article, we leverage a continuous bi-dimensional emotion space to enhance emotion representations and the training process of deep learning models. Drawing inspiration from existing categorical and dimensional emotion representations, we introduce a new representation, Dimensional Distribution Emotion State (DDES), along with a pipeline for multi-dataset training. We show that DDES provides multiple advantages compared to widely used representations while exhibiting similar baseline performance.

</details>


### [8] [VesselSim: learning 3D blood vessel segmentation without expert annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26277)
*Erin Rainville,Melissa Ananian,Tristan Mirolla,Hassan Rivax,Yiming Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VesselSim框架，仅用合成数据训练3D血管分割模型，通过测试时自适应策略实现零样本泛化到真实临床数据，性能媲美现有基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管标注依赖专家且成本高昂，严重阻碍深度学习在血管分割中的进展，亟需无需真实标注数据的训练方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 随机几何驱动的血管仿真（递归分支、曲率控制、碰撞感知拓扑）结合域随机化强度合成，生成16,500个体积数据；2) 3D U-Net在合成数据上训练，推理时通过自监督掩码重建解码器进行测试时自适应，弥合合成-真实域差距。

Result: 在多个真实MR/CT数据集（脑部、肾脏等）的零样本评估中，性能与当前最先进的血管分割基础模型相当。

Conclusion: 学习合成管状结构的几何特征可有效实现跨域鲁棒泛化，大幅减少对真实医学影像数据和专家标注的依赖。

Abstract: Blood vessel segmentation is a core task in medical image analysis for the care of vascular diseases and surgical planning, yet the challenges of providing expert vascular annotations pose a major obstacle for the progress of related deep learning techniques. To address this, we propose VesselSim, a two-stage framework for universal 3D blood vessel segmentation that eliminates the need for real annotated data during training. First, we introduce a stochastic, geometry-driven vascular simulation framework that models recursive branching, curvature-controlled growth, and collision-aware topology, followed by domain-randomized intensity synthesis to generate 16,500 anatomically plausible 3D angiographic volumes. Second, a 3D U-Net is trained solely on this synthetic data. To bridge the domain gap from synthetic to real images at inference time, we introduce a test-time adaptation strategy via a self-supervised mask reconstruction decoder, enabling adaptation to unseen clinical scans without prior domain knowledge. We evaluate VesselSim in a zero-shot setting on multiple real-world datasets spanning MR and CT across several anatomical regions, including the brain and kidneys. Despite being trained exclusively on synthetic data, VesselSim achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art vascular segmentation foundation models. These findings suggest that learning vessel geometry from synthetic tubular structures is effective for robust cross-domain generalization, substantially reducing the reliance on acquired medical imaging data and more importantly, expert annotations.

</details>


### [9] [Benchmarking Convolutional, Transformer, Hybrid, and Vision Language Models for Multi Disease Retinal Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26283)
*Durjoy Dey,Aymane Ajbar,Yuhong Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四类深度学习模型（CNN、Vision Transformer、混合CNN-Transformer、视觉-语言模型）在视网膜多疾病筛查任务上的表现，使用RFMiD数据集进行二分类筛查和28类多标签分类，并在Messidor-2上进行外部验证。结果表明，基于注意力机制的模型（如SwinTiny、CoAtNet0、MaxViTTiny）性能最佳，视觉-语言模型虽与CNN基线相当，但未超越顶尖的Transformer和混合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习在自动化视网膜筛查中广泛应用，但不同视觉模型家族在多疾病场景及域迁移下的表现尚不明确，缺乏系统的标准化对比研究。

Method: 使用RFMiD数据集，对12种架构（涵盖CNN、ViT、混合模型和视觉-语言模型）进行标准化训练、校准和评估，在二分类筛查和28类多标签分类任务上比较AUC、F1、精确率、召回率和敏感性等指标，并设置约80%特异度的临床相关操作点。

Result: 所有模型在二分类筛查上AUC均高于84%；SwinTiny、CoAtNet0和MaxViTTiny在二分类和多标签任务上均表现最佳；视觉-语言模型（CLIP ViT-B/16、SigLIP-Base384）与CNN基线相当但未超越最优模型；外部验证中AUC范围为66.8%-84.7%，混合和Transformer模型表现更强。

Conclusion: 该研究为多疾病视网膜筛查中的模型选择提供了可复现的参考基准，指导未来临床部署的自动化筛查工具设计。

Abstract: Modern deep learning offers powerful tools for automated retinal screening, but it remains unclear how different visual model families compare in realistic multi-disease settings and under domain shift. In this work, we benchmark twelve architectures across four model families: convolutional neural networks, vision transformers, hybrid CNN-transformer backbones, and vision-language models, using the Retinal Fundus Multi-disease Image Dataset (RFMiD). We evaluate two tasks: binary screening for any retinal disease and multi-label classification across 28 disease classes. Using standardized training, calibration, and evaluation protocols, we report AUC, F1, precision, recall, and sensitivity at a clinically relevant operating point with specificity near 80%. On RFMiD, all architectures perform well on binary screening, with AUC above 84%, but attention-based models perform best. SwinTiny and the hybrid CoAtNet0 and MaxViTTiny models achieve the strongest binary screening results and improve macro and micro F1 in the multi-label setting. Vision-language models, including CLIP ViT-B/16 and SigLIP-Base384, are competitive with CNN baselines but do not surpass the best transformer and hybrid backbones. In external validation on Messidor-2 for referable diabetic retinopathy, AUC ranges from 66.8% to 84.7%, with hybrid and transformer models again showing strong performance. These results provide a reproducible reference for model selection in multi-disease retinal screening and guide future automated screening tools for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [10] [A multifractal-based masked auto-encoder: an application to medical images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26287)
*Joao Batista Florindo,Viviane de Moura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多重分形分析（Renyi熵）优化掩码策略的掩码自编码器（MO-MAE），在医学图像分类任务中优于现有基线模型，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MAE的随机掩码策略可能忽略医学图像中的关键区域（细微变化即可指示疾病），需要一种能够聚焦高复杂度、高信息含量区域的掩码方法。

Method: 利用多重分形测度（Renyi熵）识别图像中复杂度高、信息量大的区域，并将掩码过程集中于此，使模型学习重建最具诊断相关性的特征。

Result: 在MedMNIST、COVID-CT等多个医学数据集上，MO-MAE超越其他基线模型和最先进方法，且计算开销极低。

Conclusion: 多重分形优化的掩码策略增强了模型捕捉和重建复杂组织结构的能力，为医学图像分析中深度学习模型的准确性和效率提升提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Masked autoencoders (MAE) have shown great promise in medical image classification. However, the random masking strategy employed by traditional MAEs may overlook critical areas in medical images, where even subtle changes can indicate disease. To address this limitation, we propose a novel approach that utilizes a multifractal measure (Renyi entropy) to optimize the masking strategy. Our method, termed Multifractal-Optimized Masked Autoencoder (MO-MAE), employs a multifractal analysis to identify regions of high complexity and information content. By focusing the masking process on these areas, MO-MAE ensures that the model learns to reconstruct the most diagnostically relevant features. This approach is particularly beneficial for medical imaging, where fine-grained inspection of tissue structures is crucial for accurate diagnosis. We evaluate MO-MAE on several medical datasets covering various diseases, including MedMNIST and COVID-CT. Our results demonstrate that MO-MAE achieves promising performance, surpassing other basiline and state-of-the-art models. The proposed method also adds minimum computational overhead as the computation of the proposed measure is straightforward. Our findings suggest that the multifractal-optimized masking strategy enhances the model's ability to capture and reconstruct complex tissue structures, leading to more accurate and efficient medical image representation. The proposed MO-MAE framework offers a promising direction for improving the accuracy and efficiency of deep learning models in medical image analysis, potentially advancing the field of computer-aided diagnosis.

</details>


### [11] [Evi-Steer: Learning to Steer Biomedical Vision-Language Models through Efficient and Generalizable Evidential Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26292)
*Taha Koleilat,Hassan Rivaz,Yiming Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evi-Steer是一个针对BiomedCLIP的不确定性感知跨模态低维微调框架，仅更新0.11%参数，在15个医学影像数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言基础模型在医学图像理解中缺乏不确定性感知能力，在域偏移和低数据场景下表现脆弱，无法满足临床对鲁棒性的要求。

Method: 在视觉和文本编码器中进行轻量低维token更新并同时估计认知不确定性；基于Dempster-Shafer理论实现跨模态置信度融合，根据文本置信度调节视觉适配并抑制冲突更新。

Result: 在15个涵盖8种器官和8种成像模态的数据集上，Evi-Steer在少样本学习和域泛化设置下均一致优于最先进方法，且仅更新0.11%的参数。

Conclusion: Evi-Steer为视觉-语言模型在真实临床场景中的部署提供了一种实用且鲁棒的路径。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient adaptation of vision-language foundation models is crucial for precise multimodal understanding of biomedical images, yet existing methods remain deterministic and often struggle under domain shift or ambiguous image-text alignment. This limitation is particularly critical in the clinic, where models should remain robust in low-data regimes and domain shifts. We present Evi-Steer, an evidential cross-modal low-dimensional steering framework for BiomedCLIP that enables uncertainty-aware parameter-efficient fine-tuning while updating only 0.11% of total model parameters. Our approach performs lightweight low-dimensional token updates in both vision and text encoders while simultaneously estimating epistemic uncertainty. These uncertainty estimates update gate residuals, allowing the model to adapt conservatively when evidence is weak. Furthermore, we introduce cross-modal confidence fusion based on Dempster-Shafer theory, enabling visual adaptation to be conditioned on textual confidence and suppressing conflicting or uncertain cross-modal updates. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation on 15 biomedical imaging datasets spanning 8 organs and 8 imaging modalities under few-shot learning and domain generalization settings. Evi-Steer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods under few-shot learning and domain shift settings, demonstrating a practical and robust pathway for deploying vision-language models in real-world clinical settings. Code is available at https://github.com/HealthX-Lab/Evi-Steer.

</details>


### [12] [Sleep-stage efficient classification using a lightweight self-supervised model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26295)
*Eldiane Borges dos Santos Durães,João Batista Florindo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种结合简化版 mulEEG 自监督模型与 Linear SVM 分类器的睡眠阶段分类方法，通过替换 ResNet-50 为 ResNet-18 并测试不同特征组合，在减少数据量的情况下取得了更优的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠阶段准确分类对诊断睡眠障碍至关重要，自动化该过程可显著提升临床评估效率。现有方法仍有改进空间，因此探索更高效的自监督模型与分类器组合。

Method: 采用简化版 mulEEG 模型（将 ResNet-50 替换为 ResNet-18 作为时间序列编码器），结合 Linear SVM 分类器。进行了两组实验：1）评估不同模型配置与数据量；2）测试时间特征、频谱特征及其拼接作为 SVM 输入的效果。

Result: 减少数据量比简化模型具有更好的成本效益比；使用 ResNet-18 提取的拼接特征超越了原始 mulEEG 的线性评估，取得了更高的分类性能。

Conclusion: 简化 mulEEG 模型提取特征并结合强分类器可实现更高效、更准确的睡眠阶段分类，该方法有望改善临床睡眠评估，并可推广至其他生物信号分类任务。

Abstract: Accurate classification of sleep stages is crucial for diagnosing sleep disorders and automating this process can significantly enhance clinical assessments. This study aims to explore the use of a self-supervised model (more specifically, an adapted version of mulEEG) combined with a Linear SVM classifier to improve sleep stage classification. \textbf{Methods:} The mulEEG model, which learns electroencephalogram signal representations in a self-supervised manner, was simplified here by replacing ResNet-50 with 1D-convolutions used as time series encoder by a ResNet-18 backbone. Two other adaptations were conducted: the first one evaluated different configurations of the model and data volume for training, while the second tested the effectiveness of time series features, spectrogram features, and their concatenation as inputs to a Linear SVM classifier. \textbf{Results:} The results showed that reducing the volume of data offered a better cost-benefit ratio compared to simplifying the model. Using the concatenated features with ResNet-18 also outperformed the linear evaluations of the original mulEEG model, achieving higher classification performance. \textbf{Conclusions:} Simplifying the mulEEG model to extract features and pairing it with a robust classifier leads to more efficient and accurate sleep stage classification. This approach holds promise for improving clinical sleep assessments and can be extended to other biological signal classification tasks.

</details>


### [13] [E$^3$C: Video Generation with 3D Environmental Memory and Ego-Exo Human Pose Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26316)
*Qiao Gu,Lingni Ma,Adam W Harley,Richard Newcombe,Florian Shkurti,Julian Straub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: E$^3$C是一个可控的以自我为中心的视频扩散框架，通过构建半密集点云3D记忆和分离的人体动态控制，实现了高保真、物理合理的自我中心视频生成，支持直观的场景编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频生成对于具身智能体理解自身及他人行为如何影响世界至关重要，但现有方法难以处理快速变化的视角、频繁的自遮挡、细微的关节动作以及人物与场景状态的一致演化。

Method: 提出E$^3$C框架，从上下文帧构建半密集点云3D记忆，并用视频VAE特征增强点外观描述；通过渲染记忆生成目标视角条件。人体动态分离建模：外部人物由骨架渲染控制（外源控制），相机佩戴者由3D关节和6DoF手腕运动控制（自我控制）。引入自我运动编码器生成持久交叉注意力token以处理不可见身体部位。

Result: 在Nymeria数据集上的实验表明，E$^3$C在视觉保真度、相机运动准确性、物体一致性以及自我/外源人体控制方面均优于强基线方法，并支持直观的场景编辑。

Conclusion: E$^3$C通过解耦持久场景结构与人类驱动动态，有效解决了自我中心视频生成中的关键挑战，为具身智能体的环境推理提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Controllable and physically grounded egocentric video generation is essential for embodied agents to reason about how their own and others' actions manifest and change the world. Compared to generic video synthesis, egocentric generation is especially challenging: the camera is tightly coupled to the actor, leading to rapid viewpoint changes and frequent self-occlusions; the underlying actions are subtle, articulated, and often only partially visible; and both the people and the scene state must evolve consistently with the specified controls. We present E$^3$C, a controllable video diffusion framework for egocentric generation that builds structured and compact conditions disentangling persistent scene structure from human-driven dynamics. From context frames, E$^3$C constructs a semi-dense point cloud-based 3D memory and augments each point with appearance descriptors from video-VAE features. Rendering this memory into target viewpoints produces conditioning aligned with the target frames. Human dynamics are modeled separately. The observed people in the scene are controlled by skeleton renderings (exo human control), while the camera wearer is specified by their 3D body joints and 6DoF wrist motion (ego human control). To preserve ego human control when the wearer's body parts are invisible, we introduce an ego motion encoder that produces persistent cross-attention tokens. Experiments on Nymeria show that E$^3$C improves visual fidelity, camera-motion accuracy, object consistency, and ego & exo human control over strong baselines, while also enabling intuitive scene editing.

</details>


### [14] [RadarSim: Simulating Single-Chip Radar via Multimodal Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26328)
*Chuhan Chen,Tianshu Huang,Akarsh Prabhakara,Chaithanya Kumar Mummadi,Zhongxiao Cong,Anthony Rowe,Matthew O'Toole,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RadarSim，一种利用RGB相机高角分辨率从相机初始化的神经场生成多普勒雷达距离图像的统一可微渲染器，能生成比纯雷达重建更清晰的几何和多普勒距离帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达数据难解释且传感器间差异大，依赖仿真；现有雷达重建方法受限于雷达低空间分辨率。

Method: 提出统一可微渲染器RadarSim，利用RGB相机高角分辨率，从相机初始化的神经场生成多普勒雷达距离图像。

Result: 在自制手持设备校准雷达相机数据集上，RadarSim生成的几何和多普勒距离帧比纯雷达重建更清晰。

Conclusion: 相机辅助可有效提升雷达重建的空间分辨率和质量。

Abstract: Radars are an ideal complement to cameras: both are inexpensive, solid-state sensors, with cameras offering fine angular resolution, while radars provide metric depth and robustness under adverse weather. However, radar data is more difficult to interpret than camera images and varies significantly between sensors, necessitating increased reliance on simulation for prototyping sensors and processing pipelines. Recent work treating radar reconstruction as a novel view synthesis problem has shown great promise in reconstructing radar-relevant geometry and simulating low-level radar data. However, such methods are constrained by the low spatial resolution of the underlying radar. To address this, we propose a unified differentiable renderer, RadarSim, which leverages the high angular resolution of RGB cameras to generate Doppler radar range images from a camera-initialized neural field. Using a novel data set of calibrated radar camera recordings from a custom hand-held rig, we demonstrate that RadarSim produces sharper geometry and Doppler range frames than radar-only reconstructions.

</details>


### [15] [Erased but Exploitable: Black-box Embedding-Aware Prompting Against Unlearned Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26332)
*Arian Komaei Koma,Seyed Amir Kasaei,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为BEAP的黑盒对抗性提示攻击方法，利用大语言模型生成隐藏的对抗提示，使文本到图像扩散模型生成应被遗忘的概念，相比先前方法攻击成功率提升超60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘攻击方法存在不现实的威胁模型假设（如白盒访问模型权重）或生成容易被检测的乱码对抗提示，无法在真实场景中有效攻击。

Method: BEAP利用大语言模型迭代生成对抗提示，在文本空间中进行嵌入感知搜索，联合优化遗忘概念存在性、文本-图像对齐和图像质量多个奖励信号。

Result: 攻击成功率比先前方法提升超过60%，每次成功攻击平均仅需15个提示，生成的对抗提示难以被安全过滤器检测。

Conclusion: BEAP在黑盒场景下有效且隐蔽地攻击了机器遗忘模型，揭示了现有安全防护的脆弱性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove specific concepts from pretrained text-to-image diffusion models, yet several white- and black-box attacks have been introduced to make the model generate such unlearned concepts. These attacks, nevertheless, do not assume a realistic threat model, i.e. they either assume access to the model weights, or result in gibberish adversarial prompts that could be easily detected even through naive rule-based safeguarding. We aim to address this gap in this paper.
  We introduce BEAP, a black-box, embedding-aware adversarial prompting attack that leverages a large language model (LLM) to iteratively generate effective adversarial prompts and exploit such hidden vulnerabilities.
  BEAP performs an embedding-aware search in text space, combining multiple reward signals: unlearned concept presence, text-image alignment, and image quality, to refine generated prompts.
  Unlike previous attack methods, BEAP keeps its prompts undetectable to safety filters while producing high-quality images.
  Extensive experiments show that BEAP improves the Attack Success Rate (ASR) by more than 60% over prior methods, while requiring only an average of fifteen prompts per successful attack. Warning: This paper contains model outputs that may be offensive or upsetting in nature.

</details>


### [16] [Personalized Generative Models for Contextual Debiasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26353)
*Xinran Liang,Esin Tureci,Prachi Sinha,Ye Zhu,Vikram V. Ramaswamy,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DecoupleGen方法，利用文本到图像扩散模型生成罕见上下文场景的图像，作为训练数据增强手段，提升模型在不常见场景下的物体识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中不同视觉模式出现频率不均，导致模型在常见场景下表现好，但在罕见场景（如沙滩球出现在路上）下识别能力不足。由于收集真实罕见场景图像困难，探索用生成图像作为训练增强。

Method: 提出DecoupleGen方法，个性化文本到图像扩散模型，在保持与原始数据集分布接近的同时，生成具有罕见上下文且视觉细节一致的图像。并应用验证约束确保增强数据的相关性。

Result: 在复杂场景数据集的物体分类和识别任务上，DecoupleGen方法持续优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 生成罕见上下文图像可有效缓解视觉模式频率不均导致的模型偏差，提升模型在不常见场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Different visual patterns appear with different frequencies in the world: e.g., beach balls appear on sand more often than they do on a road. These statistics are reflected in vision datasets, and as a result trained models more easily recognize objects in common scenarios. However, recognizing a beach ball on a road may arguably be even more important than recognizing it on sand. We study how to mitigate this discrepancy. Since collecting uncommon images in the real world may be difficult, we explore whether generating images with less frequent contexts can serve as effective training augmentation. A key challenge is guiding generations to remain close to the original dataset distribution while creating diverse images with uncommon contexts. We introduce Decoupling Contextual Patterns with Generations (DecoupleGen), a method that personalizes text-to-image diffusion models to facilitate coherent synthesis of images with rare contexts while preserving original visual details. The generated images contain semantically meaningful content and remain visually aligned with the original datasets. We further apply verification constraints to ensure relevance of the augmented data. We evaluate our approach on object classification and recognition tasks on complex scene datasets. Our experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over previous approaches, and our analyses identify factors underlying these improvements.

</details>


### [17] [Unified Panoramic Geometry Estimation via Multi-View Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26368)
*Vukasin Bozic,Isidora Slavkovic,Dominik Narnhofer,Nando Metzger,Denis Rozumny,Konrad Schindler,Nikolai Kalischek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaGeR框架利用预训练的3D基础模型，将其扩展到全景领域，实现从单张全景图像中恢复360度场景的几何结构（深度、法线、天空掩码），在室内外场景中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型主要针对透视图像设计，缺乏对全景图像（360度）的几何重建能力。将透视模型的强大先验扩展到全景领域，实现单张全景图的完整场景重建。

Method: 基于预训练的3D重建Transformer，最小化架构改动，通过混合透视图像和全景图像联合训练，使模型同时支持透视和全向输入，输出尺度不变深度、度量深度、表面法线和天空掩码。

Result: 在室内外场景中均达到SOTA性能，并展现出优秀的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: PaGeR成功将透视图像的3D基础模型能力迁移到全景领域，以极小的架构变化实现了高质量的全景几何重建，具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Geometry estimation from perspective images has greatly advanced, maturing to the point where off-the-shelf foundation models are able to reconstruct 3D scene structure not only from multi-view imagery, but even from a single view. A natural extension is 3D reconstruction from panoramas, with the exciting prospect of recovering a full 360-degree scene from a single panoramic image. In this work, we introduce PaGeR (Panoramic Geometry Reconstruction), a framework to lift powerful 3D foundation models designed for perspective imagery to the panorama domain. Our strategy is to start from a pre-trained transformer for 3D reconstruction and turn it into a unified high-performance model that predicts scale-invariant depth, metric depth, surface normals, and sky masks from both perspective and omnidirectional images, in a single forward pass. By keeping architectural changes to a minimum and mixing perspective and panoramic images during training, PaGeR retains the rich 3D prior of the underlying foundation model while learning to also estimate geometrically consistent 360-degree scenes from single panoramas. We extensively test our method in both indoor and outdoor environments and find that it delivers state-of-the-art performance and excellent zero-shot performance across a wide range of scenes.

</details>


### [18] [BioFact-MoE: Biologically Factorized Mixture of Experts for Vision-Language Prognostic Modeling in Hepatocellular Carcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26376)
*Junlin Yang,Tian Yu,Nicha C. Dvornek,Yuexi Du,Peiyu Duan,Annabella Shewarega,Lawrence H. Staib,James S. Duncan,Julius Chapiro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BioFact-MoE框架，通过生物因子化的混合专家模型将肝脏功能与肿瘤因素显式解耦，在肝细胞癌生存预测中优于基线方法，并实现了具有生物学可解释性的风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HCC预后视觉-语言模型将肝脏功能与肿瘤因素混合在单一潜变量中，限制了预测精度和生物学可解释性。

Method: 提出BioFact-MoE框架，采用生物因子化的混合专家架构，通过残差MoE生存模型显式分解肝脏和肿瘤因子，在大规模3D MRI影像-报告对上预训练。

Result: 在588例HCC队列中，12/18/24个月AUC分别达75.33%、75.85%、73.96%，优于所有基线方法；肝脏和肿瘤嵌入分别与肝功能及肿瘤负荷标志物选择性关联（p<0.05）。

Conclusion: BioFact-MoE通过显式分解生物因子，既提升了预后预测性能，又提供了具有临床意义的表型感知风险分层和治疗异质性分析能力。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is biologically heterogeneous, shaped by the interplay between hepatic functional reserve and tumor-related oncologic factors; thus, similar survival outcomes may reflect fundamentally different underlying biological processes. Prognostic modeling in HCC is informed by rich multimodal information from multiparametric MRI and radiology reports from routine clinical practice. Existing prognostic vision-language models (VLMs) learn a single entangled latent representation that blends hepatic and tumor-related factors, limiting both accuracy and biological interpretability. We present BioFact-MoE, a biologically factorized Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework that explicitly decomposes liver and tumor factors via biologically supervised experts within a residual MoE survival architecture. On a HCC cohort of N=588 patients (pretrained on 4,582 3D MRI image-report pairs), BioFact-MoE consistently improves survival prediction over all baselines across time horizons, achieving 12-, 18-, and 24-month AUCs of 75.33%, 75.85%, and 73.96%. Beyond scalar risk prediction, gated expert weights enable phenotype-aware risk stratification. Pathway-informed gating uncovers clinically meaningful treatment-associated survival heterogeneity. In held-out validation, hepatic and tumor embeddings show selective associations with liver function and tumor burden markers, respectively (p<0.05), without supervision. The code is available at https://github.com/jy-639/BioFact-MoE.

</details>


### [19] [VisualNeedle: Benchmarking Active Visual Search in Information-Dense Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26380)
*Jingru Chen,Yiming Liu,Mingtao Chen,Sijie Chen,Richeng Xuan,Liang Yang,Zhichao Hu,Fanyang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了前沿多模态大模型在细粒度感知基准测试中存在的捷径问题（语言先验、全局语义、工具伪相关），并提出VisualNeedle基准和反事实crop-black设置来评估模型真正的主动视觉搜索能力。实验表明，现有最佳模型准确率仅56.01%，远低于人类的63.00%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在细粒度感知基准上虽取得高准确率，但存在利用语言先验、全局语义和工具伪相关等捷径来“作弊”的问题，并未真正依赖视觉证据。因此需要设计一个能迫使模型进行真实主动视觉搜索的基准。

Method: 提出VisualNeedle基准，包含关键证据被限制在极小区域且无法一目了然的信息密集场景。提出反事实crop-black设置，将工具返回的裁剪区域替换为相同尺寸的黑图，以检验模型是否真正依赖中间视觉证据。在无工具、标准工具和crop-black三种设置下评估9个主流MLLM。

Result: 无工具设置下准确率低于20%，最佳工具模型仅56.01%，落后于人类多数投票的63.00%。crop-black消融实验证实，成功依赖于真实的中间视觉证据。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在细粒度视觉搜索方面存在根本性局限，提升输入分辨率或扩大问题池无法触发真正的主动视觉搜索。VisualNeedbe可作为评估模型真实视觉感知能力的有效基准。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have been reported to achieve over 90% accuracy on fine-grained perception benchmarks. However, such scores do not necessarily imply faithful use of visual evidence. Prior studies have identified three shortcuts that inflate benchmark performance. First, linguistic priors and lexical cues in questions often enable models to infer plausible answers without seeing the image. Second, coarse global semantics from the visual encoder can bypass fine-grained local details. Third, in some ``think-with-images'' benchmarks, corrupting the intermediate images returned by visual tools barely affects the final answer. These findings suggest that higher input resolution or larger question pools alone do not elicit genuine active visual search. To address this, we introduce VisualNeedle, a challenging, information-dense, and fine-grained benchmark for scenes where critical evidence is spatially constrained to minute regions and not discernible at a glance. We further propose a counterfactual crop-black setting, which replaces crops returned by tools with black images of the same size, to test whether tool-enabled performance truly relies on intermediate visual evidence. We evaluate 9 promninent MLLMs across three settings: no-tool, standard tool-enabled, and crop-black. No-tool accuracy stays below 20\%, and the best tool-enabled model reaches only 56.01\%, still trailing the 63.00% human majority-vote accuracy. These results reveal persistent limitations in fine-grained visual search, while the crop-black ablation confirms that success on VisualNeedle hinges on genuine intermediate visual evidence.

</details>


### [20] [Detail Consistent Stage-Wise Distillation for Efficient 3D MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26382)
*Mengchen Fan,Baocheng Geng,Xi Xiao,Tianyang Wang,Siyuan Mei,Pulin Che,Xiaoqian Jiang,Qizhen Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Detail Consistent Distillation (DCD)，一种在编码器各阶段通过小波域特征对齐保留结构细节的蒸馏框架，用于压缩3D医学图像分割模型，在BraTS 2024和ISLES 2022上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D医学图像分割模型（如nnU-Net）因内存和延迟问题需要压缩，但紧凑的3D编码器在多次下采样中容易丢失细小结构信息（如小病灶和锐利边界）。

Method: 提出DCD框架，在编码器每一阶段进行蒸馏，通过在**小波域**中对齐教师与学生特征的**方向性细节分量**，同时宽松约束粗尺度近似部分，避免过度正则化全局语义。该框架仅用于训练，不增加推理开销。

Result: 在BraTS 2024和ISLES 2022两个MRI基准数据集上，使用3D多模态数据取得了优越的分割性能。

Conclusion: DCD是一种有效的训练阶段蒸馏方法，能在保持结构细节的同时压缩3D医学图像分割模型，代码已开源。

Abstract: Deploying high-performing 3D medical image segmenters (e.g., nnU-Net) is often limited by memory footprint and inference latency. Compression is therefore necessary, but compact 3D encoders tend to lose fine structural cues (small lesions and sharp boundaries) as downsampling repeats across multi-resolution stages. We propose Detail Consistent Distillation (DCD), a stage-wise distillation framework that preserves structural detail across scales by aligning teacher-student features in a wavelet-decomposed representation. At each encoder stage, DCD distills directional detail components in the wavelet domain while leaving the coarse approximation comparatively unconstrained, avoiding over-regularization of global semantics. DCD is used only during training and introduces no inference-time overhead. Experiments on the BraTS 2024 and ISLES 2022 benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance in MRI segmentation using 3D multi-modal data. Code and implementation details for DCD are publicly available at https://github.com/ClinicaAlpha/DCD-3D-MedSeg.

</details>


### [21] [Zero-Shot Object Re-Identification in Egocentric Kitchen Videos via Multi-Stage SAM3 Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26383)
*Dmytro Klepachevskyi,Alexander Wong,Sirisha Rambhatla,Yuhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于自我中心视角的厨房视频中零样本物体重识别（ReID）问题。当前最先进的零样本方法（CLIP、DINOv2等）效果不佳（最高45.3% mAP），作者提出一种基于SAM3的多阶段增强流水线（融合SAM3、DINOv2、CLIP特征，结合Mask形状IoU和k-reciprocal重排序），将mAP提升至52.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心厨房视频中物体ReID面临视角快速变化、频繁遮挡、场景杂乱、类内外观差异大等问题。有监督方法难以扩展，因此需要零样本方法；但现有零样本方法在EPIC-Kitchens基准上表现不佳。

Method: 提出增强SAM3 ReID流水线，包含四个阶段：1) 使用SAM3分割抑制背景杂乱；2) 融合SAM3、DINOv2、CLIP嵌入得到L2归一化描述子；3) 在余弦相似度基础上增补mask形状IoU以实现几何一致性；4) 应用k-reciprocal重排序。

Result: 在EPIC-Kitchens基准上，最佳基线（零样本方法）仅达45.3% mAP；所提方法达到52.8% mAP，提升7.5%。

Conclusion: 零样本物体ReID在自我中心厨房视频中仍有挑战，但通过多策略融合（分割抑制背景、多模态特征融合、几何一致性约束）可显著改善性能。

Abstract: Object re-identification (ReID) in egocentric kitchen videos is challenging due to rapid viewpoint changes, frequent occlusions, cluttered scenes, and large intra-class appearance variations. Objects may leave and re-enter the field of view, and the large diversity of instances with limited annotations makes supervised ReID difficult to scale, motivating zero-shot approaches. We study zero-shot object ReID on the EPIC-Kitchens benchmark, where the goal is to match active food and kitchen-tool instances across frames using only pre-trained visual features. We first evaluate five state-of-the-art feature extractors, including Vision-Language Models (VLMs) - CLIP, DINOv2, DreamSim, I-JEPA, and SAM3 - and show that zero-shot methods fail, with the best baseline achieving only 45.3% mAP. We then propose an Enhanced SAM3 ReID Pipeline, a zero-shot multi-stage method built around SAM3 segmentation as the core component. Stage 1 uses SAM3 to suppress background clutter. Stage 2 fuses embeddings from SAM3, DINOv2, and CLIP into a single L2-normalized descriptor. Stage 3 augments cosine similarity with mask-shape IoU for geometric consistency, and Stage 4 applies k-reciprocal re-ranking. The full pipeline improves performance by 7.5% mAP to 52.8%.

</details>


### [22] [OmniGF: A Dual-Branch Vision-Language Framework for Unified Gaze Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26399)
*Qiaomu Miao,Haoyu Wu,Jingyi Xu,Minh Hoai,Dimitris Samaras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniGF是一个统一视觉-语言框架，通过双分支解码策略（语言分支+连续空间分支）结合头嵌入表示，实现了高可扩展的多人体态凝视推理，在空间定位、语义预测和社交推理任务上均达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凝视跟随模型仅局限于纯空间定位，缺乏对语义目标或复杂社交场景的高层推理能力；多人体态处理需顺序操作导致冗余计算；而VLM虽具备语义推理能力，但离散文本生成在连续空间定位任务中精度有限。

Method: 提出OmniGF框架，采用双分支解码策略：1）结构化语言分支生成离散推理状态；2）连续空间分支利用VLM密集隐藏状态，通过高分辨率凝视目标热图监督克服文本坐标生成的空间瓶颈。同时，输入增强编码了裁剪头部图像的头嵌入，为所有个体同时提供细粒度外观和朝向线索。

Result: 在多个标准基准上取得了新的SOTA性能，有效整合了精确空间凝视估计、语义凝视预测和复杂社交凝视推理。

Conclusion: OmniGF成功弥合了VLM语义推理与连续空间定位之间的鸿沟，实现了多人体态场景下的统一凝视理解。代码已开源。

Abstract: Understanding human gaze behavior is essential for complex scene comprehension and human-computer interaction. Traditional gaze following models are typically restricted to pure spatial localization, lacking the high-level capacity to reason about semantic targets or complex social contexts. Furthermore, these models often process individuals sequentially, requiring redundant computations over the same scene image for multi-person inference. While recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer the exceptional semantic reasoning needed to address gaze-related semantic tasks, their reliance on discrete text generation inherently limits precision in continuous spatial tasks like gaze localization. To bridge this gap, we propose OmniGF, a unified vision-language framework that adapts foundational VLMs for highly scalable multi-person gaze reasoning. The model adopts a dual-branch decoding strategy: a structured language branch generates discrete reasoning states, while a continuous spatial branch directly taps into the VLM's dense hidden states. Supervising these extracted representations with high-resolution gaze target heatmaps effectively overcomes the spatial bottleneck of text-only coordinate generation. Furthermore, to explicitly ground the model in multi-person scenes, we augment the input with head embeddings encoded from cropped head images, providing fine-grained appearance and orientation cues for all individuals simultaneously. By modeling all individuals and leveraging the strong semantic capability of VLMs, OmniGF seamlessly integrates precise spatial gaze target estimation, semantic gaze prediction, and complex social gaze reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework establishes new state-of-the-art performance across multiple standard benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/cvlab-stonybrook/omnigf.

</details>


### [23] [The Rescue Effect: Spatio-Semantic Early Exit Bypasses Quantization Collapse in CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26415)
*Kahyeon Nam,Hyesong Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉语言模型在INT8量化下的表征崩溃问题，提出LRA-EE早期退出方法，通过绕过噪声饱和的深层、全局补丁聚合、多特征门控和层自适应阈值，在降低计算量的同时提升零样本分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INT8量化在CLIP等联合嵌入架构中会导致激活噪声随层累积，破坏多模态嵌入的余弦对齐，造成量化诱导的表征崩溃（QIRC），而这一问题与量化CNN分类器不同，尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出LRA-EE（层感知表征早期退出）方法，包含三部分：1）空间语义聚合（用全局补丁均值替代浅层[CLS]标记）；2）学习型多特征门控（置信度、top-2边界、空间激活方差）；3）基于各层信息噪声比的层自适应置信度阈值。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K零样本分类中，相比INT8基线，FLOPs降低13.4%，Top-1准确率提升2.44%（从58.72%到61.16%）。四象限分解显示，9.5%的样本在浅层正确但被深层噪声淹没，仅7.1%相反。

Conclusion: LRA-EE能有效解决INT8量化下CLIP模型的表征崩溃问题，通过早期退出策略避免噪声饱和层的负面影响，实现计算效率与精度的双提升。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language Models on resource-constrained hardware typically requires INT8 quantization, but in joint-embedding architectures such as CLIP this introduces a failure mode distinct from quantized CNN classifiers: activation noise accumulated across transformer blocks perturbs the direction of the multimodal embedding, eroding the cosine alignment on which zero-shot retrieval depends. We characterize this as Quantization-Induced Representation Collapse (QIRC) and quantify it on INT8 CLIP ViT-B/32, where the layer-wise noise-to-signal ratio grows from below 10% in shallow blocks to 52% at Layer 11. We propose LRA-EE (Layer-wise Representation-Aware Early Exit), which bypasses noise-saturated deep layers via Spatio-Semantic Aggregation (replacing the immature shallow [CLS] with a global patch-token average), a learned multi-feature gate (confidence, top-2 margin, spatial-activation variance), and Layer-adaptive Confidence Thresholding calibrated to each layer's Information-to-Noise Ratio. On ImageNet-1K zero-shot classification, LRA-EE reduces FLOPs by 13.4% and improves Top-1 accuracy by +2.44%p (58.72% -> 61.16%) over the INT8 baseline. A four-quadrant decomposition isolates the Rescue Effect: 9.5% of samples are correctly classified at shallow exits but lost to noise at full depth, against only 7.1% suffering the inverse.

</details>


### [24] [HydraPrompt: An Adaptive and Asymmetric Framework of Vision-Language Models for Synthetic Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26421)
*Senyuan Shi,Hao Tan,Zichang Tan,Shuhan Feng,Ajian Liu,Sergio Escalera,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HydraPrompt，一种非对称提示框架，通过为真实类别使用固定提示、为伪造类别使用样本自适应提示，并引入条件监督对比学习，实现了对合成图像检测的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的可学习文本提示方法使用静态提示作为真假图像的固定边界，无法适应推理过程中出现的各种伪造类型。

Method: 提出非对称提示适配器(APA)：真实类别使用单组统一锚定提示，伪造类别构建样本自适应提示；并引入条件监督对比(CSC)目标来增强判别力。

Result: 在多个主流SID基准上取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: HydraPrompt通过动态调整类别中心、对齐细粒度图像线索，有效解决了静态提示无法适应多样伪造类型的问题。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of generative models has precipitated a proliferation of fabricated content, posing significant challenges to existing Synthetic Image Detection (SID) methods. Capitalizing on advancements in vision-language models (e.g., CLIP), recent attempts have leveraged learnable textual prompts to identify synthetic images. However, they still leverage static prompt as a fixed boundary for real and fake images, failing to adapt to the varying types of forgery that emerge during inference. To overcome this issue, we propose **HydraPrompt**, an asymmetric prompting framework that dynamically adjusts the category centers by aligning with fine-grained image cues. Specifically, we propose an Asymmetric Prompt Adapter (**APA**): (1) for authentic category, we introduce a single set of prompts to capture the consistent representative patterns, which serves as a unified anchor for real content. While (2) for fake category, we construct sample-adaptive prompts that specialize in capturing diverse cues from different samples, enabling adaptive modeling of forgery image variations. To increase pronounced discriminability within different synthetic images, we further introduce a Conditional Supervised Contrastive (**CSC**) objective, which compacts the authentic representations while capturing fine-grained forgery clues. Extensive experiments on popular SID benchmarks demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of our framework.

</details>


### [25] [Rethinking Weakly-supervised Video Temporal Grounding From a Game Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26441)
*Xiang Fang,Zeyu Xiong,Wanlong Fang,Xiaoye Qu,Chen Chen,Jianfeng Dong,Keke Tang,Pan Zhou,Yu Cheng,Daizong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将弱监督视频时间定位任务从博弈论视角建模，通过多元合作博弈理论将视频帧和查询词视为博弈玩家，学习它们对跨模态相似度得分的贡献，避免了传统方法中粗粒度学习和复杂片段提案的问题，在两个数据集上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督视频时间定位方法存在两个被忽视的问题：1) 粗粒度跨模态学习——仅捕获全局视频级对齐，无法建模帧与词之间的细粒度一致性；2) 依赖复杂的片段提案，耗时且选择困难。

Method: 将视频帧和查询词建模为博弈玩家，利用多元合作博弈理论学习它们对跨模态相似度得分的贡献；通过博弈论交互量化帧-词在联盟中的合作趋势，评估所有不确定但可能的帧-词对应关系；最终利用学习到的查询引导的帧级得分进行时刻定位，无需片段提案。

Result: 在Charades-STA和ActivityNet Caption数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: 从博弈论视角出发，通过多层级跨模态交互和灵活组合，有效解决了弱监督视频时间定位中的粗粒度学习和复杂提案依赖问题。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenging task of weakly-supervised video temporal grounding. Existing approaches are generally based on the moment proposal selection framework that utilizes contrastive learning and reconstruction paradigm for scoring the pre-defined moment proposals. Although they have achieved significant progress, we argue that their current frameworks have overlooked two indispensable issues: 1) Coarse-grained cross-modal learning: previous methods solely capture the global video-level alignment with the query, failing to model the detailed consistency between video frames and query words for accurately grounding the moment boundaries. 2) Complex moment proposals: their performance severely relies on the quality of proposals, which are also time-consuming and complicated for selection. To this end, in this paper, we make the first attempt to tackle this task from a novel game perspective, which effectively learns the uncertain relationship between each vision-language pair with diverse granularity and flexible combination for multi-level cross-modal interaction.Specifically, we creatively model each video frame and query word as game players with multivariate cooperative game theory to learn their contribution to the cross-modal similarity score. By quantifying the trend of frame-word cooperation within a coalition via the game-theoretic interaction, we are able to value all uncertain but possible correspondence between frames and words. Finally, instead of using moment proposals, we utilize the learned query-guided frame-wise scores for better moment localization.Experiments show that our method achieves superior performance on both Charades-STA and ActivityNet Caption datasets.

</details>


### [26] [Underwater360: Reconstructing Underwater Scenes from Panoramic Images with Omnidirectional Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26447)
*Jiangbei Hu,Weichao Song,Shibo Yu,Mohan Wang,Zihan Yi,Rui Wu,Mingkang Xiang,Na Lei,Shengfa Wang,Zhongxuan Luo,Ying He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Underwater360框架，结合全景成像与3D高斯泼溅（3DGS），通过球面光线追踪、物理介质建模和全景水下数据集，实现水下场景的高质量新视角合成与外观恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下场景因吸收、散射等介质效应及传统相机有限的视场角，重建极具挑战。现有3DGS在全景水下场景中存在球面投影畸变和介质退化问题。

Method: 1) 全向高斯泼溅模块：直接在球面相机空间进行光线投射，避免2D投影近似带来的几何畸变；2) 物理感知外观-介质建模架构：使用姿态条件外观嵌入，显式解耦场景固有辐射与深度依赖的后向散射和衰减；3) 构建了包含合成与真实场景的全景水下基准数据集。

Result: 在水下新视角合成和场景外观恢复任务中表现优异，渲染质量和跨视角一致性均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Underwater360有效解决了全景水下场景重建中的畸变与介质退化问题，为水下沉浸式探索提供了有效的物理信息驱动的解决方案。

Abstract: Underwater scene reconstruction is essential for immersive exploration of aquatic environments, yet remains challenging due to complex participating-media effects such as absorption and scattering, as well as the limited field of view (FoV) of conventional cameras. Although combining panoramic imaging with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers a promising direction for photorealistic underwater rendering, traditional 3DGS struggles with both spherical projection distortion and underwater medium degradation. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Underwater360}, a physics-informed omnidirectional 3DGS framework for underwater panoramic scene reconstruction. First, we introduce an Omnidirectional Gaussian Splatting module that performs ray casting directly in spherical camera space instead of relying on 2D projection approximations, thereby reducing geometric distortions under 360$^\circ$ FoV. Second, we design a physics-based appearance-medium modeling architecture with pose-conditioned appearance embeddings to explicitly decouple intrinsic scene radiance from depth-dependent backscatter and attenuation, enabling physically grounded scene appearance restoration. Finally, we establish a new panoramic underwater benchmark dataset containing both synthetic and real-world scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Underwater360 achieves superior performance in underwater novel view synthesis and scene appearance restoration, delivering improved rendering quality and cross-view consistency in complex underwater environments. The code and datasets are released at https://github.com/SwcK423/Underwater360

</details>


### [27] [Cross-scale Aligned Supervision for Training GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26449)
*Sangeek Hyun,MinKyu Lee,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有多尺度GAN中的“跨尺度轨迹错位”问题，并提出CAT（跨尺度对齐Transformer）通过生成器端的一致性正则化来对齐中间输出与最终输出，在ImageNet-256上以1.56 FID-50K取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有尺度级对抗监督并未构建真正的粗到细层级结构：中间图像各自独立逼近真实分布，无法保证跨阶段输出代表同一生成样本，且前阶段输出未被用作后阶段的显式细化目标，导致跨尺度轨迹错位问题。

Method: 保持判别器尺度级（各中间输出在其分辨率下被判别），同时在生成器侧添加一致性正则化，强制对齐中间输出与最终输出，确保跨阶段的样本轨迹一致性。

Result: 在类条件ImageNet-256上，CAT-H/2仅训练60个epoch、单步推理即达到FID-50K为1.56，超越强的一步GAN和扩散/流模型基线。

Conclusion: 验证了跨尺度轨迹对齐对多尺度生成的重要性，CAT通过简单的一致性正则化有效解决了该问题，实现了高效且高质量的一步生成。

Abstract: Modern GANs often introduce adversarial supervision on intermediate generator outputs and interpret the resulting multi-stage synthesis as coarse-to-fine hierarchical generation. In this work, we challenge this interpretation. We argue that standard scale-wise adversarial supervision does not construct a proper coarse-to-fine hierarchy: each intermediate image is independently pushed toward the real distribution at its own resolution, but this scale-wise realism does not ensure that outputs across stages represent the identical generated sample. Moreover, the scale-specific image produced at each stage is not used as an explicit refinement target for the subsequent stage. Therefore, its adversarial loss can improve a scale-specific output without constraining later stages to preserve the same sample trajectory, allowing them to move toward a different sample rather than refine the previous output. We refer to this problem as a cross-scale trajectory misalignment problem. To resolve it, we propose CAT, a Cross-scale Aligned Transformer for multi-scale adversarial generation. CAT keeps the discriminator scale-wise, so each intermediate output is evaluated at its own resolution, while adding a simple generator-side consistency regularization that aligns intermediate outputs with the final output. On class-conditional ImageNet-256, CAT-H/2 achieves an FID-50K of 1.56 with one-step inference after only 60 training epochs, outperforming strong one-step GAN and diffusion/flow baselines.

</details>


### [28] [Sparse-LiDAR Prompting of Monocular Geometry Foundations: An Empirical Study Toward Long-Range Driving Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26456)
*Kai Zheng,Qiang Feng,Xingjian Liu,Wenquan Tan,Yuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLIM 首次将 MoGe-2 点图基础模型适配为接受真正稀疏的 LiDAR 输入，通过部分卷积稀疏编码器和多尺度融合颈部，在远距离驾驶场景（100-150 米）中将绝对相对误差降低约 39-51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏 LiDAR 提示的深度基础模型（如 PromptDA、Prior Depth Anything）存在两个局限：一是缺乏对远距离（50-150 米）的系统性分层评估，二是基于视差的基础模型依赖预插值的密集先验，而真正稀疏 LiDAR 注入到点图基础模型（如 MoGe-2）尚未被探索。

Method: SLIM 将 MoGe-2 的点图解码器进行适配，引入部分卷积稀疏编码器处理真正稀疏的 LiDAR 输入，并通过多尺度融合颈部在五个尺度上将 LiDAR 特征融合到点图解码器中。采用密度无关训练策略，随机注入比例在 [0.005, 0.30] 之间。

Result: 在 Virtual KITTI 和 CARLA 上，SLIM 在 100-150 米范围内将 MoGe-2 基线的绝对相对误差降低约 39-51%。消融实验表明，部分卷积注入在 Virtual KITTI 上所有六种注入比例下均改进了 AbsRel 和 RMSE；在 CARLA 上，AbsRel 在六种设置中有五种改进，RMSE 在三种设置下提升。

Conclusion: SLIM 成功验证了在点图基础模型上进行真正稀疏 LiDAR 注入的有效性，显著提升了远距离深度估计性能，且单一模型可适应不同输入密度。

Abstract: Sparse-LiDAR-prompted depth foundation models (PromptDA, Prior Depth Anything, DMD3C) have shown strong results on indoor scenes or within KITTI's standard 80-meter evaluation cap. However, two limitations remain: (i) systematic distance-stratified evaluation in long-range driving regimes (50-150 m) is largely absent; (ii) prior approaches built on disparity-based foundations rely on pre-interpolated dense priors, leaving truly sparse LiDAR injection on point-map foundations (e.g., MoGe-2, NeurIPS 2025) unexplored. We present SLIM (Sparse-LiDAR Injected Monocular geometry), the first adaptation of MoGe-2 to accept truly sparse LiDAR input. SLIM integrates a partial-convolution sparse encoder with a multi-scale fusion neck that fuses LiDAR features into the point-map decoder at five scales. We adopt density-agnostic training (random injection ratio in [0.005, 0.30]) so a single model serves diverse input densities. On Virtual KITTI and CARLA, SLIM reduces the absolute relative error of the MoGe-2 baseline by approximately 39-51% at 100-150 m. Ablation across six injection ratios shows partial-convolution injection improves both AbsRel and RMSE on Virtual KITTI in all six settings; on CARLA, AbsRel improves in five of six settings (one near-tie at 0.015 differs by 0.0013), and RMSE is comparable across encoders, with partial-convolution improving in three settings (by up to 0.31 unit) and losing by at most 0.11 unit in the other three.

</details>


### [29] [AnchorDiff: Training-Free Concept Grounding for MM-DiTs via Anchor-Based Graph Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26460)
*Jian Zhang,Zhijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorDiff是一种无需训练的语义定位方法，通过锚点选择与混合图传播解耦策略，有效缓解多模态扩散Transformer中视觉相似概念间的激活重叠问题（即概念泄漏）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的方法在视觉易混淆概念上会产生重叠激活（概念泄漏），导致目标响应溢出到非目标对象上。

Method: 从概念到图像的注意力图中选取高置信度锚点，将其作为独热种子在图像自注意力导出的混合图上传播；利用输出空间相似性进行密集的类内传播，并通过行级注意力门控抑制跨对象连接。

Result: 在ImageNet-Segmentation和PascalVOC上表现优异，并在所提出的多概念混淆数据集上显著减少了概念泄漏。

Conclusion: AnchorDiff通过解耦语义定位与结构精化，有效缓解了视觉相似概念间的概念泄漏问题，提升了无需训练的语义定位性能。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) encode rich representations for training-free concept grounding, but existing attention-based methods often produce overlapping activations on visually confusable concepts, a failure mode we call concept leakage, where target responses spill over to non-target objects. To address this issue, we propose AnchorDiff, a training-free grounding method that decouples semantic localization from structural refinement. AnchorDiff selects a high-confidence anchor from concept-to-image attention map and propagates it as a one-hot seed over a hybrid graph derived from image-to-image self-attention. The graph uses output-space similarity for dense within-object propagation and a row-wise attention gate to suppress cross-object connections. Additionally, we introduce the Multi-Concept Confusion Dataset, which contains images with multiple visually similar concepts and separate masks, enabling explicit evaluation of concept leakage. Experiments show that AnchorDiff achieves strong grounding performance on ImageNet-Segmentation and PascalVOC, while substantially reducing concept leakage on our Multi-Concept Confusion Dataset.

</details>


### [30] [Triadic Dynamics Aware Diffusion Posterior Sampling for Inverse Problems: Optimizing Guidance and Stochasticity Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26470)
*Junseo Bang,Dong Ju Mun,Hoigi Seo,Seongmin Hong,Se Young Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TriPS方法，通过优化数据一致性引导、无分类器引导和随机性三个组件的时间调度策略，显著提升了扩散模型在成像逆问题中的后验采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对三个核心组件（数据一致性引导、无分类器引导、随机性）的调度策略多是启发式固定或部分调整的次优方案，缺乏对三者相互作用及调度影响的系统性研究。

Method: 将后验采样重新表述为时变控制问题，提出TriPS方法：1）基于模板搜索获得基线调度方案；2）利用GRPO强化学习优化灵活的时间曲线，遵循递减DC和随机性尺度、递增CFG尺度的三态趋势。

Result: 在数据保真度和感知真实度方面，TriPS优于现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 三个组件之间的调度交互对逆问题求解性能至关重要，所提出的三态动态感知调度策略能有效提升扩散模型后验采样的效果。

Abstract: Generative posterior sampling using diffusion models has emerged as a dominant paradigm for solving inverse problems in imaging, which usually consists of three main components: data consistency (DC) guidance, classifier-free guidance (CFG) and stochasticity. While prior arts have focused on how to develop each or all components, less attention has given to how to schedule them, leading to heuristically fixed or partially adjusted suboptimal schedules. In this work, we argue that the interactions among all three components in terms of scheduling are crucial for significantly improved performance in solving inverse problems in imaging. Our analysis shows that aggressive CFG early in sampling conflict with DC guidance, while stochasticity brings the trajectory back to higher-probability regions. Based on these findings, we propose Triadic Dynamics Aware Posterior Sampling (TriPS), which reformulates posterior sampling as a time-varying control problem and optimizes schedules following a triadic trend of decreasing DC and stochasticity scales alongside increasing CFG scale. TriPS achieves this through two strategies: template-based search over functional priors for reliable baseline schedules, and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)-based reinforcement learning for more flexible temporal curves. Experiments demonstrate TriPS outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in data fidelity and perceptual realism.

</details>


### [31] [Comparative Study of Vision-Based Metric Measurement for Large-Scale Planar Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26475)
*ZhiXin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于视觉的大范围室外场景平面度量测量问题，比较了单目测距、图像拼接与双目测距三种方法，并分析了各自在俯仰角、场景规模等方面的性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型室外环境中，由于远距离感知、相机变焦和成像条件不稳定等因素，基于视觉的度量距离和面积测量仍然具有挑战性。本文旨在真实水库监控场景下系统比较三种代表性方法的性能与适用性。

Method: 采用PTZ相机，研究三种方法：(1) 基于几何模型的单目测距，推导平面定位模型并分析俯仰角影响；(2) 图像拼接结合鸟瞰图变换实现大面积地图构建；(3) 使用两个联合标定的单目相机实现立体测距，无需专用立体硬件。

Result: 单目测距在较大俯仰角下可达到米级精度；双目测距可达分米级精度，且对俯仰角变化的敏感度更低；图像拼接在小场景中效果良好，但随场景增大稳定性和可扩展性下降。

Conclusion: 三种方法各有优劣，实际应用需根据场景规模、精度要求和俯仰角条件进行选择：单目适用于大俯仰角场景，双目适用于高精度需求，图像拼接适用于小范围场景。

Abstract: Vision-based metric distance and area measurement remains challenging in large-scale outdoor environments due to long-range sensing, camera zoom, and unstable imaging conditions. This work studies planar metric measurement in a real-world reservoir monitoring scenario using PTZ cameras and compares three representative approaches: geometry-based monocular ranging, image stitching with birds-eye-view transformation, and stereo-based ranging using two jointly calibrated monocular cameras. For monocular ranging, planar localization models are derived from camera geometry and the effect of camera pitch angle is analyzed. Image stitching is investigated for large-area mapping, while a stereo-based scheme is developed for long-range measurement without dedicated stereo hardware. Experiments show clear trade-offs: monocular ranging achieves meter-level accuracy under sufficiently large pitch angles, stereo-based ranging achieves decimeter-level accuracy with reduced sensitivity to pitch variations, and image stitching is effective for small-scale scenes but degrades in stability and scalability as scene size increases.

</details>


### [32] [Clinically-Grounded Counterfactual Reasoning for Medical Video Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26483)
*Jianzhe Gao,Churan Wang,Weiyi Zhang,Jianghua Li,Li-An Li,Wenguan Wang,Yixin Zhu,Yizhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedVCR是一个基于反事实推理的医学视频诊断框架，通过扩散模型生成反事实组织演化图像，结合临床规则学习和双重诊断策略，在宫颈镜和结肠镜视频诊断任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视频诊断方法存在三大问题：只关注外观而非病理特征、缺乏临床先验知识、仅基于观察推理而无反事实对比。为模拟临床医生的诊断思维方式而提出MedVCR。

Method: MedVCR包含三个组件：1) 反事实生成器：基于扩散模型合成指定病理状态下的组织演化图像；2) 反事实表示学习：通过时间一致性、病理可分离性和反事实对齐等临床规则编码诊断知识；3) 双重诊断预测：结合视频级评估和帧级反事实分析。

Result: 在全监督（宫颈镜）和弱监督（结肠镜）视频诊断场景下，相比主流基线方法取得2.6%-10.2%的性能提升，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: MedVCR通过引入反事实推理机制有效提升了医学视频诊断性能，更贴近临床诊断思维，代码将开源。

Abstract: Medical video diagnosis involves inferring clinical decisions from dynamic tissue responses throughout examination processes. Existing methods rely on an end-to-end learning paradigm that i) focuses on appearance rather than pathology, ii) lacks clinical priors, and iii) reasons solely from observations without counterfactual comparison. This work introduces MedVCR, a counterfactual reasoning framework that mimics clinical diagnostic thinking. MedVCR comprises three components: a Counterfactual Generator that synthesizes tissue evolution under specified pathological states via a diffusion-based manner; a Counterfactual Representation Learning module that encodes diagnostic knowledge through clinical rules (i.e., temporal consistency, pathological separability, and counterfactual alignment); and a Dual Diagnostic Prediction strategy that integrates video-level assessment with frame-level counterfactual analysis. MedVCR is evaluated under both fully supervised (e.g., colposcopy) and weakly supervised (e.g., colonoscopy) video diagnosis settings, yielding 2.6%-10.2% performance gains compared with leading baselines. Comprehensive ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each component. The code will be released.

</details>


### [33] [OmniInteract: Benchmarking Real-World Streaming Interaction for Real-Time Omnimodal Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26485)
*Xudong Lu,Xueying Li,Annan Wang,Yang Bo,Jinpeng Chen,Zengliang Li,Nianzu Yang,Rui Liu,Xue Yang,Jingwen Hou,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniInteract是一个用于评估实时全模态大语言模型在音视频流中在线推理能力的流式基准测试，包含250个视频和1430个时间定位响应槽，实验表明当前模型在流式交互中表现较弱，最佳IA-QTF1仅0.368。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试多为离线视频理解或文本提示的流式QA，无法评估模型在真实音视频流中的在线交互能力，用户查询和环境音嵌入音频轨道需要模型检测多模态触发信号、决定何时响应并实时作答。

Method: 构建包含250个视频、1430个时间定位响应槽的基准测试，分为1Q1A（1062个，含实时、主动、嵌套场景）和1QnA（368个，用于连续任务监控与步骤指导），使用IA-QTF1、中断诊断套件和嵌套链完成分数评估。

Result: 当前最佳整体IA-QTF1仅0.368，最佳1QnA IA-QTF1仅0.052；全双工数学推理实验表明离线能力不一定迁移到在线交互。

Conclusion: 现有模型在流式交互任务中表现薄弱，亟需针对在线流式交互能力的提升研究。

Abstract: We introduce OmniInteract, a streaming benchmark for real-time omnimodal large language models evaluated through native online inference over audio-visual streams. Unlike offline video understanding or text-prompted streaming QA, OmniInteract preserves the original audio-visual stream and requires models to process it online, without access to future content. User queries and ambient sounds are embedded in the audio track, requiring models to detect multimodal triggers, decide when to respond, and answer while the stream unfolds. OmniInteract contains 250 videos with 1,430 temporally grounded response slots: 1,062 1Q1A slots across real-time, proactive, and nested scenarios, and 368 1QnA slots for continuous task monitoring and step guidance. Each slot includes a trigger, response window, and target answer. We evaluate response correctness, timing, invalid outputs, interruption handling, and context continuity using Interaction-Aware Quality-Timeliness F1, Interruption Diagnostic Suite, and Nested Chain Completion Score. Experiments show that current models remain weak in streaming interaction, with the best overall IA-QTF1 reaching only 0.368 and the best 1QnA IA-QTF1 only 0.052. Further study on mathematical reasoning in full-duplex settings shows that offline capability does not necessarily transfer to online interaction. Code and datasets will be made publicly accessible at https://github.com/Lucky-Lance/OmniInteract.

</details>


### [34] [LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26486)
*Meituan LongCat Team,Xunliang Cai,Meng Cheng,Feng Gao,Zhe Kong,Jiamu Li,Le Li,Weiheng Li,Hongyu Liu,Shuai Tan,Xiaoming Wei,Tianyu Yang,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5 是一个升级版开源框架，通过改进音频编码器、训练配方、数据筛选和RLHF训练，实现了高精度口型同步、全身时间稳定性和长视频生成，并支持多人物交互和物体处理。通过步进蒸馏将推理加速至8 NFE，在500+测试案例上达到或超越HeyGen、OmniHuman 1.5等商业系统，弥合了学术原型与商业部署之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管音频驱动视频生成技术已有进展，但实现商业级稳定性仍然具有挑战性。现有系统在口型同步、全身稳定性、长视频生成以及工业部署效率等方面存在不足，亟需一个开源且生产就绪的解决方案。

Method: 1) 将音频编码器升级为Whisper Large；2) 精心缩放训练配方；3) 严格的数据筛选和RLHF训练；4) 采用高级步进蒸馏将推理加速至8 NFE。

Result: 在500+多样化测试案例的基准上，通过定量指标和人工评估，LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5在人类相似度评分和专家级质量评估上达到或超越了HeyGen、OmniHuman 1.5、Kling Avatar 2.0等领先闭源系统。

Conclusion: LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5通过系统工程优化而非架构创新，实现了商业级性能，其开源发布缩小了学术研究原型与商业部署之间的差距。

Abstract: Despite advances in audio-driven video generation, achieving commercial-grade stability remains challenging. We present LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5, an upgraded open-source framework prioritizing systematic engineering and production-readiness over architectural novelty. By upgrading the audio encoder to Whisper Large and meticulously scaling our training recipes, v1.5 achieves accurate lip-synchronization, full-body temporal stability, and robust long-video generation with strict identity consistency. Through rigorous data curation and RLHF Training, the model readily generalizes to stylized domains such as anime and animals, and natively handles complex real-world conditions, such as multi-person interactions and object handling. Furthermore, addressing the practical demands of industrial deployment, we employ advanced step distillation to accelerate inference to an optimal 8 NFE, achieving a favorable trade-off between serving efficiency and visual fidelity. The superiority of our approach is validated through extensive quantitative metrics and a rigorous human evaluation conducted on a comprehensive benchmark of over 500 diverse test cases. Results show that v1.5 achieves competitive or superior performance compared to leading closed-source systems (e.g., HeyGen, OmniHuman 1.5, Kling Avatar 2.0) across human-likeness ratings and expert-level quality assessments on our benchmark. With its open-source release, LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5 narrows the gap between academic research prototypes and commercial-grade deployment.

</details>


### [35] [3D Gaussian Map with Open-Set Semantic Grouping for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26500)
*Jianzhe Gao,Rui Liu,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D高斯地图（3D Gaussian Map）用于视觉-语言导航（VLN），通过在线构建以3D高斯原语表示的场景地图，并结合开放集语义分组与多粒度动作预测策略，在R2R、R4R、REVERIE三个公开基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法虽引入多种场景表示以增强空间感知，但忽略了复杂3D几何结构与丰富语义信息，导致在多样未见环境中的泛化能力受限。

Method: 1) 从稀疏伪激光雷达点云初始化3D高斯，在线构建自我中心场景地图；2) 通过开放集语义分组操作，基于实例/类别归属对高斯原语进行分组，形成统一的3D高斯地图；3) 设计多层级动作预测策略，融合多粒度空间-语义线索辅助决策。

Result: 在R2R、R4R、REVERIE三个公开基准上的大量实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的3D高斯地图能够有效整合几何与语义信息，提升VLN智能体在复杂未知环境中的导航泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an agent to traverse complex 3D environments based on natural language instructions, necessitating a thorough scene understanding. While existing works equip agents with various scene representations to enhance spatial awareness, they often neglect the complex 3D geometry and rich semantics in VLN scenarios, limiting the ability to generalize across diverse and unseen environments. To address these challenges, this work proposes a 3D Gaussian Map that represents the environment as a set of differentiable 3D Gaussians and accordingly develops a navigation strategy for VLN. Specifically, Egocentric Scene Map is constructed online by initializing 3D Gaussians from sparse pseudo-lidar point clouds, providing informative geometric priors for scene understanding. Each Gaussian primitive is further enriched through Open-Set Semantic Grouping operation, which groups 3D Gaussians based on their membership in object instances or stuff categories within the open world, resulting in a unified 3D Gaussian Map. Building on this map, Multi-Level Action Prediction strategy, which combines spatial-semantic cues at multiple granularities, is designed to assist agents in decision-making. Extensive experiments conducted on three public benchmarks (i.e., R2R, R4R, and REVERIE) validate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [36] [Unveiling the Fragility of Vision-Language Models: Multi-Modal Adversarial Synergy via Texture-Constrained Perturbations and Cross-Modal Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26501)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Changshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态对抗协同框架（MMAS），通过联合优化图像通用对抗扰动（基于小波纹理约束）和文本可学习提示扰动（基于嵌入空间L范数约束），在仅使用模型查询的黑盒设置下实现对大型视觉语言模型的高效通用攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM对抗攻击研究主要针对单一模态或需要白盒访问，缺乏同时利用双模态且具备黑盒实用性的通用攻击方法，无法充分评估LVLM在真实场景中的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出MMAS框架：1) 图像侧：小波纹理约束的通用对抗扰动，确保视觉不可感知性；2) 文本侧：嵌入空间L范数约束的可学习提示扰动，保持语义连贯性；3) 跨模态正则化项对齐梯度方向，增强协同效应与迁移性。所有优化仅依赖模型查询（黑盒）。

Result: 在主流LVLM上展现出强大的通用对抗攻击能力，验证了方法的有效性与跨模型/跨任务迁移性。

Conclusion: MMAS填补了LVLM双模态黑盒通用攻击的空白，揭示了多模态融合范式的安全漏洞，为构建更鲁棒的视觉语言模型提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have transformed multi-modal understanding, excelling in tasks like image captioning and visual question answering by integrating visual and textual inputs. However, their robustness against adversarial attacks, particularly those exploiting both modalities, remains underexplored, posing risks to critical applications like autonomous driving and content moderation. Existing attacks focus on single modalities or require impractical white-box access, limiting their real-world relevance. In this paper, we introduce Multi-Modal Adversarial Synergy, a groundbreaking framework that crafts universal, black-box multi-modal attacks against LVLMs. MMAS simultaneously generates a texture scale-constrained universal adversarial perturbation for images and a learnable prompt perturbation for text, optimized jointly using only model queries. The image perturbation leverages wavelet-based texture constraints to ensure imperceptibility and robustness across diverse visual inputs. The text perturbation, constrained by an L-norm in the embedding space, maintains semantic coherence while steering outputs toward a target. A novel cross-modal regularization term aligns the perturbations' gradient directions, enhancing their synergistic impact and transferability across tasks and models. Extensive experiments show the strong universal adversarial capabilities of our proposed attack with prevalent LVLMs.

</details>


### [37] [Uncertainty-Aware Gaussian Map for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26503)
*Jianzhe Gao,Rui Liu,Yuxuan Xu,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang,Zhanguang Zhang,Sida Peng,Yi Yang,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在视觉-语言导航中显式建模三种感知不确定性（几何、语义、外观），通过构建语义高斯地图（SGM）和价值地图，使智能体能够在不确定性信息下做出更可靠的导航决策，在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN智能体在导航中常遇到感知不确定性（如证据不足、空间线索模糊），但普遍忽略这些信息直接预测动作，导致决策不可靠。

Method: 1) 构建语义高斯地图（SGM），由可微3D高斯图元组成；2) 几何不确定性通过高斯位置和尺度的变分扰动评估；3) 语义不确定性通过扰动高斯语义属性捕捉；4) 外观不确定性通过Fisher信息量刻画；5) 将不确定性整合到统一的3D价值地图中。

Result: 在多个VLN基准数据集上的全面评估表明该方法有效提升了导航性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模和利用感知不确定性能够显著改善VLN智能体在复杂3D环境中的导航可靠性和决策质量。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to navigate 3D environments following natural language instructions. During navigation, existing agents commonly encounter perceptual uncertainty, such as insufficient evidence for reliable grounding or ambiguity in interpreting spatial cues, yet they typically ignore such information when predicting actions.
